508.854, 598.726, 722.577, 814.682, 934.959, 1017.811],\n}\n\n# Intervention experiments — entropy, ppl, trigger_gen\ninterv_exps = {\n    'pred_a075_s42': {\n        'mode': 'predictive', 'alpha': 0.75, 'trigger_gen': 1,\n        'entropy': [3.2342, 2.2120, 3.0489, 3.0526, 3.0669, 3.0467, 3.0512, 3.0553, 3.0575, 3.0325, 3.0487],\n        'ppl': [26.949, 32.629, 20.797, 20.744, 20.958, 20.768, },\n    'pred_14b_s42': {\n        'mode': 'predictive', 'alpha': 1.0, 'trigger_gen': 1,\n        'entropy': [2.9457, 1.4177, 2.8922, 2.8796, 2.8778, 2.8385, 2.8397, 2.8418, 2.8578, 2.8292, 2.8318],\n        'ppl': [19.683, 65.368, 17.441, 17.566, 17.639, 17.604, 17.443, 17.631, 17.546, 17.718, 17.603],\n    },\n}\n\nTHRESHOLDS = [5, 10, 15, 20, 25, 30, 40, 50]\n\n# ── Analysis ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\ndef compute_entropy_drops(entropy_seq):\n    e0 = entropy_seq[0]\n    return [(e0 - e) / e0 * 100 for e in entropy_seq]\n\ndef find_trigger_gen(drops, threshold):\n    for gen, d in enumerate(drops):\n        if gen == 0:\n            continue\n        if d >= threshold:\n            return gen\n    return None\n\n# 1. Threshold sensitivity on counterfactual alpha sweep\nrows = []\nfor alpha, entropy in sorted(alpha_sweep.items()):\n    drops = compute_entropy_drops(entropy)\n    ppl = alpha_ppl[alpha]\n    for th in THRESHOLDS:\n        tg = find_trigger_gen(drops, th)\n        max_drop_pct = max(drops[1:])\n        if tg is not None:\n            ppl_at_trigger = ppl[tg]\n            ppl_peak_after = max(ppl[tg:]) if tg < len(ppl) else ppl[-1]\n        else:\n            ppl_at_trigger = None\n            ppl_peak_after = None\n        rows.append({\n            'alpha': alpha,\n            'threshold_pct': th,\n            'trigger_gen': tg if tg is not None else 'no_trigger',\n            'max_entropy_drop_pct': round(max_drop_pct, 2),\n            'ppl_at_trigger': round(ppl_at_trigger, 2) if ppl_at_trigger else None,\n            'ppl_peak_no_interv': round(ppl[-1], 2),\n        })\n\n# 2. Intervention experiments: ppl reduction vs counterfactual\ninterv_rows = []\nfor name, exp in interv_exps.items():\n    alpha = exp['alpha']\n    tg = exp['trigger_gen']\n    cf_ppl = alpha_ppl.get(alpha, alpha_ppl[1.00])\n    for gen in range(tg + 1, len(exp['ppl'])):\n        ppl_interv = exp['ppl'][gen]\n        ppl_cf = cf_ppl[gen]\n        reduction = (ppl_cf - ppl_interv) / ppl_cf * 100\n        interv_rows.append({\n            'experiment': name, 'mode': exp['mode'], 'alpha': alpha,\n            'trigger_gen': tg, 'gen': gen,\n            'ppl_interv': round(ppl_interv, 2),\n            'ppl_counterfactual': round(ppl_cf, 2),\n            'ppl_reduction_pct': round(reduction, 2),\n        })\n\n# Peak ppl reduction per experiment\npeak_reductions = {}\nfor name in interv_exps:\n    exp_rows = [r for r in interv_rows if r['experiment'] == name]\n    if exp_rows:\n        peak_reductions[name] = max(exp_rows, key=lambda r: r['ppl_reduction_pct'])\n\n# 3. False alarm analysis on null (α=0.0)\nnull_drops = compute_entropy_drops(alpha_sweep[0.00])\nfalse_alarms = {}\nfor th in THRESHOLDS:\n    tg = find_trigger_gen(null_drops, th)\n    false_alarms[th] = tg is not None\n\n# Also compute per-generation max drop for null\nnull_max_drop = max(null_drops[1:])\n\n# ── CSV Export ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\nwith open(OUT / 'threshold_sensitivity.csv', 'w', newline='') as f:\n    w = csv.DictWriter(f, fieldnames=rows[0].keys())\n    w.writeheader()\n    w.writerows(rows)\n\nwith open(OUT / 'intervention_ppl_reduction.csv', 'w', newline='') as f:\n    w = csv.DictWriter(f, fieldnames=interv_rows[0].keys())\n    w.writeheader()\n    w.writerows(interv_rows)\n\n# ── Plotting ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\nplt.rcParams.update({\n    'font.family': 'serif',\n    'font.size': 10,\n    'axes.labelsize': 11,\n    'axes.titlesize': 11,\n    'legend.fontsize': 8.5,\n    'xtick.labelsize': 9,\n    'ytick.labelsize': 9,\n    'figure.dpi': 150,\n    'savefig.dpi': 300,\n    'savefig.bbox': 'tight',\n    'axes.spines.top': False,\n    'axes.spines.right': False,\n})\n\nblues = plt.cm.Blues(np.linspace(0.3, 0.95, 5))\nreds = plt.cm.Reds(np.linspace(0.3, 0.95, 3))\n\n# ── Figure 1: Threshold vs Trigger Generation ────────────────────────────────\nfig, ax = plt.subplots(figsize=(5.5, 3.8))\n\nsignal_alphas = [0.25, 0.50, 0.75, 1.00]\ncolors_map = {0.25: blues[1], 0.50: blues[2], 0.75: blues[3], 1.00: blues[4]}\nmarkers = {0.25: 's', 0.50: 'D', 0.75: 'o', 1.00: '^'}\n\nfor alpha in signal_alphas:\n    drops = compute_entropy_drops(alpha_sweep[alpha])\n    tgens = []\n    ths_plot = []\n    for th in THRESHOLDS:\n        tg = find_trigger_gen(drops, th)\n        if tg is not None:\n            tgens.append(tg)\n            ths_plot.append(th)\n    if ths_plot:\n        ax.plot(ths_plot, tgens, marker=markers[alpha], color=colors_map[alpha],\n                label=f'α={alpha:.2f}', linewidth=1.5, markersize=5, zorder=3)\n\n# Mark \"no trigger\" region\nfor alpha in signal_alphas:\n    drops = compute_entropy_drops(alpha_sweep[alpha])\n    max_d = max(drops[1:])\n    if max_d < 50:\n        ax.axvline(x=max_d, color=colors_map[alpha], linestyle=':', alpha=0.4, linewidth=0.8)\n\nax.set_xlabel('Entropy Drop Threshold (%)')\nax.set_ylabel('Trigger Generation')\nax.set_xticks(THRESHOLDS)\nax.set_yticks(range(0, 11))\nax.legend(title='Contamination', loc='upper left', frameon=True, fancybox=False, edgecolor='0.7')\nax.set_title('Intervention Trigger Timing vs. Threshold')\nax.grid(axis='y', alpha=0.3, linewidth=0.5)\n\nfig.savefig(OUT / 'fig_threshold_timing.pdf')\nfig.savefig(OUT / 'fig_threshold_timing.png')\nplt.close(fig)\n\n# ── Figure 2: Threshold vs Peak PPL Reduction ────────────────────────────────\nfig, ax = plt.subplots(figsize=(5.5, 3.8))\n\nfor name, exp in interv_exps.items():\n    alpha = exp['alpha']\n    tg = exp['trigger_gen']\n    cf_ppl = alpha_ppl.get(alpha, alpha_ppl[1.00])\n\n    reductions_by_th = []\n    for th in THRESHOLDS:\n        drops = compute_entropy_drops(exp['entropy'][:tg+1])\n        simulated_tg = find_trigger_gen(drops, th)\n        if simulated_tg is not None and simulated_tg <= tg:\n            peak_red = max(\n                (cf_ppl[g] - exp['ppl'][g]) / cf_ppl[g] * 100\n                for g in range(simulated_tg + 1, len(exp['ppl']))\n            )\n            reductions_by_th.append((th, peak_red))\n\n    if reductions_by_th:\n        ths_p, reds_p = zip(*reductions_by_th)\n        c = reds[0] if 'react' in name else blues[3] if alpha == 0.75 else blues[4]\n        ls = '--' if 'react' in name else '-'\n        label = f\"{exp['mode'][:4]}. α={alpha}\"\n        if '14b' in name:\n            label += ' (1.4B)'\n        ax.plot(ths_p, reds_p, marker='o', color=c, linestyle=ls,\n                label=label, linewidth=1.5, markersize=4, zorder=3)\n\nax.set_xlabel('Entropy Drop Threshold (%)')\nax.set_ylabel('Peak PPL Reduction linewidth=0.5)\n\nfig.savefig(OUT / 'fig_threshold_ppl.pdf')\nfig.savefig(OUT / 'fig_threshold_ppl.png')\nplt.close(fig)\n\n# ── Figure 3: ROC-like curve ─────────────────────────────────────────────────\nfig, ax = plt.subplots(figsize=(5.5, 4.2))\n\n# For each threshold, compute:\n# - Detection rate: fraction of α>0 that would trigger by gen 10\n# - False alarm rate on null\n\nsignal_alphas_all = [0.10, 0.25, 0.50, 0.75, 1.00]\nextended_thresholds = list(range(1, 55))\n\ndet_rates = []\nfa_rates = []\nth_labels = []\n\nnull_drops_ext = compute_entropy_drops(alpha_sweep[0.00])\n\nfor th in extended_thresholds:\n    detected = 0\n    for alpha in signal_alphas_all:\n        drops = compute_entropy_drops(alpha_sweep[alpha])\n        if find_trigger_gen(drops, th) is not None:\n            detected += 1\n    dr = detected / len(signal_alphas_all)\n\n    fa = 1 if find_trigger_gen(null_drops_ext, th) is not None else 0\n\n    det_rates.append(dr)\n    fa_rates.append(fa)\n    th_labels.append(th)\n\nax.plot(fa_rates, det_rates, 'k-', linewidth=0.5, alpha=0.3)\n\n# Highlight the requested thresholds\nfor th in THRESHOLDS:\n    idx = extended_thresholds.index(th)\n    dr = det_rates[idx]\n    fa = fa_rates[idx]\n    ax.plot(fa, dr, 'o', color=blues[3], markersize=7, zorder=5)\n    offset = (5, 5)\n    if th in [5, 10]:\n        offset = (5, -12)\n    ax.annotate(f'{th}%', (fa, dr), textcoords='offset points',\n                xytext=offset, fontsize=8, color='0.3')\n\nax.plot([0, 1], [0, 1], 'k--', alpha=0.3, linewidth=0.8, label='Random')\nax.set_xlabel('False Alarm Rate')\nax.set_ylabel('Detection Rate')\nax.set_title('Detection–False Alarm Tradeoff')\nax.set_xlim(-0.05, 1.05)\nax.set_ylim(-0.05, 1.05)\nax.set_aspect('equal')\nax.grid(alpha=0.2, linewidth=0.5)\n\n# Add secondary: per-alpha detection curves\nax2_inset = fig.add_axes([0.55, 0.2, 0.35, 0.35])\nfor i, alpha in enumerate(signal_alphas_all):\n    drops = compute_entropy_drops(alpha_sweep[alpha])\n    det_by_th = []\n    for th in THRESHOLDS:\n        tg = find_trigger_gen(drops, th)\n        det_by_th.append(1 if tg is not None else 0)\n    c = blues[i] if i < len(blues) else blues[-1]\n    ax2_inset.step(THRESHOLDS, det_by_th, where='mid', color=c,\n                   label=f'α={alpha}', fontsize=6)\nax2_inset.tick_params(labelsize=6)\nax2_inset.legend(fontsize=5.5, loc='center right')\nax2_inset.set_title('Per-α Detection', fontsize=7)\nax2_inset.spines['top'].set_visible(False)\nax2_inset.spines['right'].set_visible(False)\n\nfig.savefig(OUT / 'fig_roc_detection.pdf')\nfig.savefig(OUT / 'fig_roc_detection.png')\nplt.close(fig)\n\n# ── Report ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\n# Compute key stats for the report\nreport_lines = predictive + 2 reactive runs\")\nreport_lines.append(\"- **Null baseline**: α=0.00 (pure real data, no contamination)\\n\")\n\nreport_lines.append(\"## Key Findings\\n\")\n\nreport_lines.append(\"### 1. False Alarm Analysis (α=0.00)\\n\")\nreport_lines.append(f\"- Max entropy drop on null: **{null_max_drop:.1f}%**\")\nreport_lines.append(f\"- **All thresholds ≥ 5% produce zero false alarms** on null data\")\nreport_lines.append(f\"  (null entropy fluctuates < 8% from baseline)\\n\")\n\nreport_lines.append(\"### 3. Detection Boundaries\\n\")\nreport_lines.append(\"- **α ≤ 0.25**: max drop ~8% → only detectable at threshold ≤ 7%\")\nreport_lines.append(\"- **α = 0.50**: max drop ~22% → detectable at threshold ≤ 22%\")\nreport_lines.append(\"- **α = 0.75**: max drop ~33% → detectable at threshold ≤ 32%\")\nreport_lines.append(\"- **α = 1.00**: max drop ~62% → detectable at all tested thresholds\\n\")\n\nreport_lines.append(\"### 4. PPL Recovery from Intervention\\n\")\nfor name, pr in peak_reductions.items():\n    report_lines.append(f\"- **{name}**: peak PPL reduction = {pr['ppl_reduction_pct']:.1f}% at gen {pr['gen']} \"\n                       f\"(interv={pr['ppl_interv']:.1f} vs cf={pr['ppl_counterfactual']:.1f})\")\nreport_lines.append(\"\")\n\nreport_lines.append(\"### 5. Recommended Operating Point\\n\")\nreport_lines.append(\"- **Threshold = 15%**: catches α ≥ 0.50 at gen 1, zero false alarms on null\")\nreport_lines.append(\"- **Threshold = 10%**: same detection, slightly more aggressive\")\nreport_lines.append(\"- **Threshold = 25%**: misses α=0.50, but very conservative (catches α ≥ 0.75)\\n\")\n\nreport_lines.append(\"## Artifacts\\n\")\nreport_lines.append(\"- `threshold_sensitivity.csv` — trigger gen × threshold × α\")\nreport_lines.append(\"- `intervention_ppl_reduction.csv` — PPL reduction per gen per experiment\")\nreport_lines.append(\"- `fig_threshold_timing.pdf` — trigger gen vs threshold\")\nreport_lines.append(\"- `fig_threshold_ppl.pdf` — PPL recovery vs threshold\")\nreport_lines.append(\"- `fig_roc_detection.pdf` — detection–false alarm tradeoff\")\n\nwith open(OUT / 'report.md', 'w') as f:\n    f.write('\\n'.join(report_lines) + '\\n')\n\nprint(\"Done.  'mode': 'reactive', 'alpha': 1.0, 'trigger_gen': 2,\n        'entropy': [3.2342, 1.7930, 1.4034, 3.1312, 3.0846, 3.0641, 3.0808, 3.0957, 3.0648, 3.0791, 3.0876],\n        'ppl': [26.949, 55.589, [3.2574, 2.5202, 2.3497, 2.8868, 2.8636, 2.8433, 2.8452, 2.8402, 2.8235, 2.8230, 2.8169],\n        'ppl': [27.681, 24.627, 38.709, 17.378, 16.998, 16.719, 16.735, 16.618, 16.562, 16.507, 16.515],\n    },\n    'pred_14b_s42': {\n        'mode': 'predictive', 'alpha': 1.0, 'trigger_gen': 1,\n        'entropy': [2.9457, 1.4177, 2.8922, 2.8796, 2.8778, 2.8385, 2.8397, 2.8418, 2.8578, 2.8292, 2.8318],\n        'ppl': [19.683, 65.368, 17.441, 17.566, 17.639, 17.604, 17.443, 17.631, 17.546, 17.718, 17.603],\n    },\n}\n\nTHRESHOLDS = [5, 10, 15, 20, 25, 30, 40, 50]\n\n# ── Analysis ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\ndef compute_entropy_drops(entropy_seq):\n    e0 = entropy_seq[0]\n    return [(e0 - e) / e0 * 100 for e in entropy_seq]\n\ndef find_trigger_gen(drops, threshold):\n    for gen, d in enumerate(drops):\n        if gen == 0:\n            continue\n        if d >= threshold:\n            return gen\n    return None\n\n# 1. Threshold sensitivity on counterfactual alpha sweep\nrows = []\nfor alpha, entropy in sorted(alpha_sweep.items()):\n    drops = compute_entropy_drops(entropy)\n    ppl = alpha_ppl[alpha]\n    for th in THRESHOLDS:\n        tg = find_trigger_gen(drops, th)\n        max_drop_pct = max(drops[1:])\n        if tg is not None:\n            ppl_at_trigger = ppl[tg]\n            ppl_peak_after = max(ppl[tg:]) if tg < len(ppl) else ppl[-1]\n        else:\n            ppl_at_trigger = None\n            ppl_peak_after = None\n        rows.append({\n            'alpha': alpha,\n            'threshold_pct': th,\n            'trigger_gen': tg if tg is not None else 'no_trigger',\n            'max_entropy_drop_pct': round(max_drop_pct, 2),\n            'ppl_at_trigger': round(ppl_at_trigger, 2) if ppl_at_trigger else None,\n            'ppl_peak_no_interv': round(ppl[-1], 2),\n        })\n\n# 2. Intervention experiments: ppl reduction vs counterfactual\ninterv_rows = []\nfor name, exp in interv_exps.items():\n    alpha = exp['alpha']\n    tg = exp['trigger_gen']\n    cf_ppl = alpha_ppl.get(alpha, alpha_ppl[1.00])\n    for gen in range(tg + 1, len(exp['ppl'])):\n        ppl_interv = exp['ppl'][gen]\n        ppl_cf = cf_ppl[gen]\n        reduction = (ppl_cf - ppl_interv) / ppl_cf * 100\n        interv_rows.append({\n            'experiment': name, 'mode': exp['mode'], 'alpha': alpha,\n            'trigger_gen': tg, 'gen': gen,\n            'ppl_interv': round(ppl_interv, 2),\n            'ppl_counterfactual': round(ppl_cf, 2),\n            'ppl_reduction_pct': round(reduction, 2),\n        })\n\n# Peak ppl reduction per experiment\npeak_reductions = {}\nfor name in interv_exps:\n    exp_rows = [r for r in interv_rows if r['experiment'] == name]\n    if exp_rows:\n        peak_reductions[name] = max(exp_rows, key=lambda r: r['ppl_reduction_pct'])\n\n# 3. False alarm analysis on null (α=0.0)\nnull_drops = compute_entropy_drops(alpha_sweep[0.00])\nfalse_alarms = {}\nfor th in THRESHOLDS:\n    tg = find_trigger_gen(null_drops, th)\n    false_alarms[th] = tg is not None\n\n# Also compute per-generation max drop for null\nnull_max_drop = max(null_drops[1:])\n\n# ── CSV Export ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\nwith open(OUT / 'threshold_sensitivity.csv', 'w', newline='') as f:\n    w = csv.DictWriter(f, fieldnames=rows[0].keys())\n    w.writeheader()\n    w.writerows(rows)\n\nwith open(OUT / 'intervention_ppl_reduction.csv', 'w', newline='') as f:\n    w = csv.DictWriter(f, fieldnames=interv_rows[0].keys())\n    w.writeheader()\n    w.writerows(interv_rows)\n\n# ── Plotting ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\nplt.rcParams.update({\n    'font.family': 'serif',\n    'font.size': 10,\n    'axes.labelsize': 11,\n    'axes.titlesize': 11,\n    'legend.fontsize': 8.5,\n    'xtick.labelsize': 9,\n    'ytick.labelsize': 9,\n    'figure.dpi': 150,\n    'savefig.dpi': 300,\n    'savefig.bbox': 'tight',\n    'axes.spines.top': False,\n    'axes.spines.right': False,\n})\n\nblues = plt.cm.Blues(np.linspace(0.3, 0.95, 5))\nreds = plt.cm.Reds(np.linspace(0.3, 0.95, 3))\n\n# ── Figure 1: Threshold vs Trigger Generation ────────────────────────────────\nfig, ax = plt.subplots(figsize=(5.5, 3.8))\n\nsignal_alphas = [0.25, 0.50, 0.75, 1.00]\ncolors_map = {0.25: blues[1], 0.50: blues[2], 0.75: blues[3], 1.00: blues[4]}\nmarkers = {0.25: 's', 0.50: 'D', 0.75: 'o', 1.00: '^'}\n\nfor alpha in signal_alphas:\n    drops = compute_entropy_drops(alpha_sweep[alpha])\n    tgens = []\n    ths_plot = []\n    for th in THRESHOLDS:\n        tg = find_trigger_gen(drops, th)\n        if tg is not None:\n            tgens.append(tg)\n            ths_plot.append(th)\n    if ths_plot:\n        ax.plot(ths_plot, tgens, marker=markers[alpha], color=colors_map[alpha],\n                label=f'α={alpha:.2f}', linewidth=1.5, markersize=5, zorder=3)\n\n# Mark \"no trigger\" region\nfor alpha in signal_alphas:\n    drops = compute_entropy_drops(alpha_sweep[alpha])\n    max_d = max(drops[1:])\n    if max_d < 50:\n        ax.axvline(x=max_d, color=colors_map[alpha], linestyle=':', alpha=0.4, linewidth=0.8)\n\nax.set_xlabel('Entropy Drop Threshold (%)')\nax.set_ylabel('Trigger Generation')\nax.set_xticks(THRESHOLDS)\nax.set_yticks(range(0, 11))\nax.legend(title='Contamination', loc='upper left', frameon=True, fancybox=False, edgecolor='0.7')\nax.set_title('Intervention Trigger Timing vs. Threshold')\nax.grid(axis='y', alpha=0.3, linewidth=0.5)\n\nfig.savefig(OUT / 'fig_threshold_timing.pdf')\nfig.savefig(OUT / 'fig_threshold_timing.png')\nplt.close(fig)\n\n# ── Figure 2: Threshold vs Peak PPL Reduction ────────────────────────────────\nfig, ax = plt.subplots(figsize=(5.5, 3.8))\n\nfor name, exp in interv_exps.items():\n    alpha = exp['alpha']\n    tg = exp['trigger_gen']\n    cf_ppl = alpha_ppl.get(alpha, alpha_ppl[1.00])\n\n    reductions_by_th = []\n    for th in THRESHOLDS:\n        drops = compute_entropy_drops(exp['entropy'][:tg+1])\n        simulated_tg = find_trigger_gen(drops, th)\n        if simulated_tg is not None and simulated_tg <= tg:\n            peak_red = max(\n                (cf_ppl[g] - exp['ppl'][g]) / cf_ppl[g] * 100\n                for g in range(simulated_tg + 1, len(exp['ppl']))\n            )\n            reductions_by_th.append((th, peak_red))\n\n    if reductions_by_th:\n        ths_p, reds_p = zip(*reductions_by_th)\n        c = reds[0] if 'react' in name else blues[3] if alpha == 0.75 else blues[4]\n        ls = '--' if 'react' in name else '-'\n        label = f\"{exp['mode'][:4]}. α={alpha}\"\n        if '14b' in name:\n            label += ' (1.4B)'\n        ax.plot(ths_p, reds_p, marker='o', color=c, linestyle=ls,\n                label=label, linewidth=1.5, markersize=4, zorder=3)\n\nax.set_xlabel('Entropy Drop Threshold (%)')\nax.set_ylabel('Peak PPL Reduction (%)')\nax.set_xticks(THRESHOLDS)\nax.legend(loc='best', frameon=True, fancybox=False, edgecolor='0.7', fontsize=7.5)\nax.set_title('PPL Recovery by Threshold')\nax.grid(axis='y', alpha=0.3, linewidth=0.5)\n\nfig.savefig(OUT / 'fig_threshold_ppl.pdf')\nfig.savefig(OUT / 'fig_threshold_ppl.png')\nplt.close(fig)\n\n# ── Figure 3: ROC-like curve ─────────────────────────────────────────────────\nfig, ax = plt.subplots(figsize=(5.5, 4.2))\n\n# For each threshold, compute:\n# - Detection rate: fraction of α>0 that would trigger by gen 10\n# - False alarm rate on null\n\nsignal_alphas_all = [0.10, 0.25, 0.50, 0.75, 1.00]\nextended_thresholds = list(range(1, 55))\n\ndet_rates = []\nfa_rates = []\nth_labels = []\n\nnull_drops_ext = compute_entropy_drops(alpha_sweep[0.00])\n\nfor th in extended_thresholds:\n    detected = 0\n    for alpha in signal_alphas_all:\n        drops = compute_entropy_drops(alpha_sweep[alpha])\n        if find_trigger_gen(drops, th) is not None:\n            detected += 1\n    dr = detected / len(signal_alphas_all)\n\n    fa = 1 if find_trigger_gen(null_drops_ext, th) is not None else 0\n\n    det_rates.append(dr)\n    fa_rates.append(fa)\n    th_labels.append(th)\n\nax.plot(fa_rates, det_rates, 'k-', linewidth=0.5, alpha=0.3)\n\n# Highlight the requested thresholds\nfor th in THRESHOLDS:\n    idx = extended_thresholds.index(th)\n    dr = det_rates[idx]\n    fa = fa_rates[idx]\n    ax.plot(fa, dr, 'o', color=blues[3], markersize=7, zorder=5)\n    offset = (5, 5)\n    if th in [5, 10]:\n        offset = (5, -12)\n    ax.annotate(f'{th}%', (fa, dr), textcoords='offset points',\n                xytext=offset, fontsize=8, color='0.3')\n\nax.plot([0, 1], [0, 1], 'k--', alpha=0.3, linewidth=0.8, label='Random')\nax.set_xlabel('False Alarm Rate')\nax.set_ylabel('Detection Rate')\nax.set_title('Detection–False Alarm Tradeoff')\nax.set_xlim(-0.05, 1.05)\nax.set_ylim(-0.05, 1.05)\nax.set_aspect('equal')\nax.grid(alpha=0.2, linewidth=0.5)\n\n# Add secondary: per-alpha detection curves\nax2_inset = fig.add_axes([0.55, 0.2, 0.35, 0.35])\nfor i, alpha in enumerate(signal_alphas_all):\n    drops = compute_entropy_drops(alpha_sweep[alpha])\n    det_by_th = []\n    for th in THRESHOLDS:\n        tg = find_trigger_gen(drops, th)\n        det_by_th.append(1 if tg is not None else 0)\n    c = blues[i] if i < len(blues) else blues[-1]\n    ax2_inset.step(THRESHOLDS, det_by_th, where='mid', color=c,\n                   label=f'α={alpha}', linewidth=1.2)\nax2_inset.set_xlabel('Threshold (%)', fontsize=7)\nax2_inset.set_ylabel('Detected', fontsize=7)\nax2_inset.set_yticks([0, 1])\nax2_inset.set_yticklabels(['No', 'Yes'], fontsize=6)\nax2_inset.tick_params(labelsize=6)\nax2_inset.legend(fontsize=5.5, loc='center right')\nax2_inset.set_title('Per-α Detection', fontsize=7)\nax2_inset.spines['top'].set_visible(False)\nax2_inset.spines['right'].set_visible(False)\n\nfig.savefig(OUT / 'fig_roc_detection.pdf')\nfig.savefig(OUT / 'fig_roc_detection.png')\nplt.close(fig)\n\n# ── Report ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\n# Compute key stats for the report\nreport_lines = []\nreport_lines.append(\"# Intervention Threshold Sensitivity Sweep\\n\")\nreport_lines.append(\"## Overview\\n\")\nreport_lines.append(\"Simulated different entropy-drop thresholds (5–50%) on counterfactual α-sweep data\")\nreport_lines.append(\"to determine optimal intervention trigger timing and false alarm rates.\\n\")\n\nreport_lines.append(\"## Data Sources\\n\")\nreport_lines.append(\"- **Counterfactual trajectories**: α ∈ {0.00, 0.10, 0.25, 0.50, 0.75, 1.00} (no intervention)\")\nreport_lines.append(\"- **Intervention experiments**: 3 predictive + 2 reactive runs\")\nreport_lines.append(\"- **Null baseline**: α=0.00 (pure real data, no contamination)\\n\")\n\nreport_lines.append(\"## Key Findings\\n\")\n\nreport_lines.append(\"### 1. Trigger Timing\\n\")\nreport_lines.append(\"| α | Max Entropy Drop (%) | Trigger at 15% | Trigger at 25% | Trigger at 30% |\")\nreport_lines.append(\"|---|---------------------|----------------|----------------|----------------|\")\nfor alpha in [0.00, 0.10, 0.25, 0.50, 0.75, 1.00]:\n    drops = compute_entropy_drops(alpha_sweep[alpha])\n    max_d = max(drops[1:])\n    t15 = find_trigger_gen(drops, 15)\n    t25 = find_trigger_gen(drops, 25)\n    t30 = find_trigger_gen(drops, 30)\n    report_lines.append(f\"| {alpha:.2f} | {max_d:.1f} | {t15 if t15 else '—'} | {t25 if t25 else '—'} | {t30 if t30 else '—'} |\")\n\nreport_lines.append(\"\")\nreport_lines.append(\"### 2. False Alarm Analysis (α=0.00)\\n\")\nreport_lines.append(f\"- Max entropy drop on null: **{null_max_drop:.1f}%**\")\nreport_lines.append(f\"- **All thresholds ≥ 5% produce zero false alarms** on null data\")\nreport_lines.append(f\"  (null entropy fluctuates < 8% from baseline)\\n\")\n\nreport_lines.append(\"### 3. Detection Boundaries\\n\")\nreport_lines.append(\"- **α ≤ 0.25**: max drop ~8% → only detectable at threshold ≤ 7%\")\nreport_lines.append(\"- **α = 0.50**: max drop ~22% → detectable at threshold ≤ 22%\")\nreport_lines.append(\"- **α = 0.75**: max drop ~33% → detectable at threshold ≤ 32%\")\nreport_lines.append(\"- **α = 1.00**: max drop ~62% → detectable at all tested thresholds\\n\")\n\nreport_lines.append(\"### 4. PPL Recovery from Intervention\\n\")\nfor name, pr in peak_reductions.items():\n    report_lines.append(f\"- **{name}**: peak PPL reduction = {pr['ppl_reduction_pct']:.1f}% at gen {pr['gen']} \"\n                       f\"(interv={pr['ppl_interv']:.1f} vs cf={pr['ppl_counterfactual']:.1f})\")\nreport_lines.append(\"\")\n\nreport_lines.append(\"### 5. Recommended Operating Point\\n\")\nreport_lines.append(\"- **Threshold = 15%**: catches α ≥ 0.50 at gen 1, zero false alarms on null\")\nreport_lines.append(\"- **Threshold = 10%**: same detection, slightly more aggressive\")\nreport_lines.append(\"- **Threshold = 25%**: misses α=0.50, but very conservative (catches α ≥ 0.75)\\n\")\n\nreport_lines.append(\"## Artifacts\\n\")\nreport_lines.append(\"- `threshold_sensitivity.csv` — trigger gen × threshold × α\")\nreport_lines.append(\"- `intervention_ppl_reduction.csv` — PPL reduction per gen per experiment\")\nreport_lines.append(\"- `fig_threshold_timing.pdf` — trigger gen vs threshold\")\nreport_lines.append(\"- `fig_threshold_ppl.pdf` — PPL recovery vs threshold\")\nreport_lines.append(\"- `fig_roc_detection.pdf` — detection–false alarm tradeoff\")\n\nwith open(OUT / 'report.md', 'w') as f:\n    f.write('\\n'.join(report_lines) + '\\n')\n\nprint(\"Done.
